Compute visible width. If <= width, return s unchanged (no tail, no extra reset). This is important: truncation only occurs when needed.\n3. Else truncate:\n   - tailWidth = ANSIWidth(opts.Tail)\n   - budget = width - tailWidth; if budget < 0 → budget = 0 (and maybe tail itself truncated? probably not; tail included as is, resulting width > width. Hmm. Could truncate tail to width. Simpler: budget = max(0, width - tailWidth), append tail fully).\n   - Iterate tokens, accumulate width; for text tokens, walk grapheme clusters/runes, add runes while total+budget not exceeded; if a rune (wide) would exceed, stop and truncate.\n   - Keep escape tokens encountered before the cut. Do not include escapes after the cut point (except final reset/close which we append ourselves).\n   - After text cut, append close for open hyperlink, then tail, then final SGR reset if styles active.\n   \n   Order: \"Append a final SGR reset if styles are active. Close open OSC 8 hyperlinks.\" Which order? Probably close hyperlink then reset, or reset then close. Terminal-wise, hyperlink close then SGR reset seems natural. Which do tests expect?
